```
78	const gPath = (idx: number, ext: string) => `${G2}/${String(idx).padStart(3, '0')}_${DUMPNAME.get(idx)}.${ext}`;
79	interface Ch { t: Uint16Array; a: Uint8Array; w: Uint16Array; l: Uint8Array; hf: Uint8Array; sl: Uint8Array; lt: Uint8Array; ia: Uint8Array }
80	const chCache = new Map<number, Ch>();
81	function chans(idx: number): Ch {
82	  const hit = chCache.get(idx);
83	  if (hit) return hit;
84	  const b8 = (e: string) => new Uint8Array(readFileSync(gPath(idx, e)).buffer.slice(0));
85	  const b16 = (e: string) => new Uint16Array(readFileSync(gPath(idx, e)).buffer.slice(0));
86	  const c: Ch = { t: b16('typ'), a: b8('act'), w: b16('wal'), l: b8('liq'), hf: b8('half'), sl: b8('slope'), lt: b8('lty'), ia: b8('inact') };
87	  chCache.set(idx, c);
88	  if (chCache.size > 8) chCache.delete(chCache.keys().next().value!);
89	  return c;
90	}

92	interface Boundary { gs: GenState; gensolid: Map<number, boolean>; fx: Uint16Array; fy: Uint16Array; wire: Uint8Array; ids35: Map<number, number>; chests: unknown[] }
93	const bCache = new Map<string, Boundary>();
94	function boundary(key: string): Boundary {
95	  const hit = bCache.get(key);
96	  if (hit) return hit;
97	  const dir = `${BDIR}/${key}`;
98	  if (!existsSync(`${dir}/gs.json`)) throw new Error(`no boundary ${key}`);
99	  const gs = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${dir}/gs.json`, 'utf8')) as GenState;
100	  const gensolid = new Map(JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${dir}/gensolid.json`, 'utf8')) as Array<[number, boolean]>);
101	  const fw = new Uint32Array(readFileSync(`${dir}/frames.bin`).buffer.slice(0));
102	  const fx = new Uint16Array(N), fy = new Uint16Array(N), wire = new Uint8Array(N);
103	  for (let q = 0; q < fw.length; q += 4) { const i = fw[q]!; fx[i] = fw[q + 1]!; fy[i] = fw[q + 2]!; wire[i] = fw[q + 3]! & 0x1f; }
104	  const iw = new Uint32Array(readFileSync(`${dir}/ids35.bin`).buffer.slice(0));
105	  const ids35 = new Map<number, number>();
106	  for (let q = 0; q < iw.length; q += 2) ids35.set(iw[q]!, iw[q + 1]!);
107	  const chests = JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${dir}/chests.json`, 'utf8')) as unknown[];
108	  const b: Boundary = { gs, gensolid, fx, fy, wire, ids35, chests };
109	  bCache.set(key, b);
110	  return b;
111	}

113	interface PrevOut { type: Uint16Array; flags: Uint8Array; fx: Uint16Array; fy: Uint16Array; wire: Uint8Array }
114	function buildHybrid(baseIdx: number, b: Boundary | PrevOut): { st: TileStore; revMiss: number } {
115	  const c = chans(baseIdx);
116	  const st = new TileStore(W, H);
117	  const fromB = 'ids35' in b;
118	  const ids35 = fromB ? (b as Boundary).ids35 : null;
119	  let revMiss = 0;
120	  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
121	    const act = c.a[i]!;
122	    st.flags[i] = act;
123	    if (act || c.t[i]) {
124	      const sh = c.t[i]!;
125	      let id = ids35 && ids35.has(i) ? ids35.get(i)! : -1;
126	      if (id < 0 || SHEET[id] !== sh) { id = REV.get(sh) ?? -1; }
127	      if (id < 0) { id = 0; revMiss++; }
128	      st.type[i] = id;
129	    } else st.type[i] = 0;
130	    st.wall[i] = c.w[i]!;
131	    st.liquid[i] = c.l[i]!;
132	    st.liquidType[i] = c.l[i] ? c.lt[i]! + 1 : 0;
133	    st.half[i] = c.hf[i]!;
134	    st.slope[i] = c.sl[i]!;
135	    st.wire[i] = c.ia[i]! ? 0x20 : 0;
136	    if (fromB) {
137	      const bb = b as Boundary;
138	      st.frameX[i] = bb.fx[i]; st.frameY[i] = bb.fy[i];
139	      st.wire[i] |= bb.wire[i];
140	    } else {
141	      const p = b as PrevOut;
142	      st.frameX[i] = p.fx[i]; st.frameY[i] = p.fy[i];
143	      st.wire[i] |= p.wire[i] & 0x1f;
144	    }
145	  }
146	  return { st, revMiss };
147	}
148	const prevOf = (st: TileStore): PrevOut => ({ type: st.type.slice(), flags: st.flags.slice(), fx: st.frameX.slice(), fy: st.frameY.slice(), wire: st.wire.slice() });

150	// ---- 八通道 diff（_mile8 cmp 口径）----
151	interface Row { idx: number; tag: string; dA: number; dT: number; dW: number; dL: number; dHf: number; dSl: number; dLt: number; dIa: number; first: string; pairs: Array<[string, number]>; samples: string[] }
152	function diffStore(tag: string, idx: number, st: TileStore): Row {
153	  const c = chans(idx);
154	  const r: Row = { idx, tag, dA: 0, dT: 0, dW: 0, dL: 0, dHf: 0, dSl: 0, dLt: 0, dIa: 0, first: '', pairs: [], samples: [] };
155	  const pair = new Map<string, number>();
156	  let firstI = -1;
157	  const mark = (i: number) => { if (firstI < 0) firstI = i; };
158	  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
159	    const va = c.a[i]!, ja = st.flags[i]! & 1;
160	    if (va !== ja) { r.dA++; mark(i); if (r.samples.length < 10) r.samples.push(`A(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${va} j${ja}`); }
161	    const vt = va ? c.t[i]! : 0;
162	    const jt = ja ? (SHEET[st.type[i]!] >= 0 ? SHEET[st.type[i]!]! : 9999) : 0;
163	    if (vt !== jt) { r.dT++; mark(i); const k = `T${vt}>${jt}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); if (r.samples.length < 10) r.samples.push(`T(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${vt} j${jt}`); }
164	    if (c.w[i]! !== st.wall[i]!) { r.dW++; mark(i); if (r.samples.length < 10) r.samples.push(`W(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.w[i]} j${st.wall[i]}`); }
165	    if (c.l[i]! !== st.liquid[i]!) { r.dL++; mark(i); if (r.samples.length < 10) r.samples.push(`L(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.l[i]} j${st.liquid[i]}`); }
166	    if (c.hf[i]! !== st.half[i]!) { r.dHf++; mark(i); const k = `H${c.hf[i]}>${st.half[i]}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); if (r.samples.length < 10) r.samples.push(`H(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.hf[i]} j${st.half[i]}`); }
167	    if (c.sl[i]! !== st.slope[i]!) { r.dSl++; mark(i); const k = `S${c.sl[i]}>${st.slope[i]}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); if (r.samples.length < 10) r.samples.push(`S(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${c.sl[i]} j${st.slope[i]}`); }
168	    const vl = c.l[i]! > 0 ? c.lt[i]! + 1 : 0;
169	    const jl = st.liquid[i]! > 0 ? st.liquidType[i]! : 0;
170	    if (vl !== jl) { r.dLt++; mark(i); const k = `Y${vl}>${jl}`; pair.set(k, (pair.get(k) ?? 0) + 1); if (r.samples.length < 10) r.samples.push(`Y(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${vl} j${jl} liq${c.l[i]}`); }
171	    const via = c.ia[i]! ? 1 : 0;
172	    const jia = (st.wire[i]! >>> 5) & 1;
173	    if (via !== jia) { r.dIa++; mark(i); if (r.samples.length < 10) r.samples.push(`I(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0}) v${via} j${jia}`); }
174	  }
175	  if (firstI >= 0) r.first = `(${firstI % W},${Math.floor(firstI / W)})`;
176	  r.pairs = [...pair.entries()].sort((a, b) => b[1] - a[1]).slice(0, Number(process.env.SW_WWW_PAIRS ?? 8)).map(([k, n]) => [k, n] as [string, number]);
177	  return r;
178	}
179	const isClean = (r: Row) => !(r.dA || r.dT || r.dW || r.dL || r.dHf || r.dSl || r.dLt || r.dIa);
180	function printRow(r: Row): void {
181	  const bad = !isClean(r);

184	    `Hf=${String(r.dHf).padStart(5)} Sl=${String(r.dSl).padStart(5)} Lt=${String(r.dLt).padStart(4)} Ia=${String(r.dIa).padStart(4)} ${bad ? '✗ ' + r.first : '✓'}`);
185	  if (bad) {
186	    console.log(`     pairs: ${r.pairs.map(([k, n]) => `${k}×${n}`).join(' ')}`);
187	    console.log(`     samples: ${r.samples.join(' | ')}`);
188	  }
189	}

200	/** 跨槽生产者重算：shrineSpots / oceanTreasures（golden47/36 干净基座 + m_ 中途边界） */
201	function computeProducers(): { shrineSpots: Array<{ x: number; y: number }>; oceanTreasures: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> } {
202	  const mj = boundary('m_JungleShrines');
203	  const { st: stJ } = buildHybrid(47, mj);
204	  restoreGenSolid(mj.gensolid);
205	  const shrineSpots = runJungleShrinePass(stJ, new RNG(SEED), structuredClone(mj.gs) as GenState);
206	  const mo = boundary('m_OceanCaves');
207	  const { st: stO } = buildHybrid(36, mo);
208	  restoreGenSolid(mo.gensolid);
209	  const oceanTreasures = runOceanCavesPass(stO, new RNG(SEED), structuredClone(mo.gs) as GenState);
210	  return { shrineSpots, oceanTreasures };
211	}

213	async function runSettleStep(st: TileStore, gs: GenState, rng: RNG, world: World): Promise<void> {
214	  world.store = st;
215	  world.shimmerX = gs.shimmerX; world.shimmerY = gs.shimmerY;
216	  world.genWaterLine = gs.waterLine;
217	  if ((gs.dungeonSide as number) === 0) world.genDungeonExempt = null;
218	  else {
219	    const edge = Math.floor(world.w * 0.05), mid = Math.floor(world.w * 0.55);
220	    world.genDungeonExempt = gs.dungeonSide < 0
221	      ? { x0: edge, x1: Math.floor(world.w * 0.45), y0: Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) + 10, y1: (world.h - 200) - 10 }
222	      : { x0: mid, x1: world.w - edge, y0: Math.floor(gs.worldSurface) + 10, y1: (world.h - 200) - 10 };
223	  }
224	  await settleWorldLiquids(world, 'gen', undefined, rng);
225	  flipGenSolid([484], false);
226	}
227	
228	// jsSlot → golden 槽步进表（JS 槽界 = s{jsSlot-1}；entry 槽的 gs/GenSolid 快照起）
229	// producersBox：shrineSpots/oceanTreasures 惰性重算后注入（闭包读盒子）
230	function makePlans(producersBox: { shrineSpots: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null; oceanTreasures: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null }): Map<number, SlotPlan> {

235	  P.set(17, { entry: 's16', steps: [{ dump: 57, base: 56, run: (st, gs, rng) => runLifeCrystalsPass(st, rng, gs) }] });
236	  P.set(18, {
237	    entry: 's17',
238	    steps: [
239	      { dump: 58, base: 57, run: (st, gs, rng) => runStatuesPass(st, rng, gs) },
240	      {
241	        // WWWW：SW_WWW_59G=all|fr,wire,chest —— #59 入口金标反事实注入
242	        // （TTTT 织入产物 059_Buried_Chests.{fr,wire,chest.tsv} = pass 头快照）。
243	        // 用于隔离 house#111 接受分歧的输入态（帧/线/箱注册表）债。
244	        dump: 59, base: 58,
245	        run: (st, gs, rng, chests) => {
246	          const g59 = (process.env.SW_WWW_59G ?? '').split(',').filter(Boolean);
247	          const on = (k: string) => g59.includes('all') || g59.includes(k);
248	          if (on('fr')) { injectGoldenFrames(st, '/tmp/tttt-fr/059_Buried_Chests.fr'); console.log('  [59G] golden frames injected'); }
249	          if (on('wire')) { injectGoldenWires(st, '/tmp/tttt-fr/059_Buried_Chests.wire'); console.log('  [59G] golden wires injected'); }
250	          if (on('chest')) {
251	            const gold = loadGoldenChests('/tmp/tttt-fr/059_Buried_Chests.chest.tsv');
252	            if (gold) { (chests as Chests).length = 0; (chests as Chests).push(...gold); console.log(`  [59G] golden chest registry injected (${gold.length})`); }
253	          }
254	          flipGenSolid([226, 162, 225, 190], true);
255	          rollHouseChestCounts(st, rng, gs);
256	          runBuriedChestsPass(st, rng, gs, chests);
257	          runUnderworldChestsPass(st, rng, gs, chests);
258	          runCaveHousesPass(st, rng, gs, chests);
259	          flipGenSolid([226, 162, 225, 190], false);
260	        },
261	      },
262	      { dump: 60, base: 59, run: (st, gs, rng, chests) => runSurfaceChestsPass(st, rng, gs, chests) },
263	      { dump: 61, base: 60, run: (st, gs, rng, chests) => runJungleChestsPass(st, rng, gs, chests, producersBox.shrineSpots!) },
264	      { dump: 62, base: 61, run: (st, gs, rng, chests) => { runUnderwaterChestsPass(st, rng, gs, chests, producersBox.oceanTreasures!); runWaterChestsPass(st, rng, gs, chests); } },
265	      {

364	  const injectGoldenFrames = (st: InstanceType<typeof TileStore>, frPath: string) => {
365	    if (!existsSync(frPath)) return;
366	    const b = readFileSync(frPath);
367	    const cnt = new DataView(b.buffer, b.byteOffset, b.byteLength).getUint32(0, true);
368	    for (let q = 0; q < cnt; q++) {
369	      const off = 4 + q * 8;
370	      const idx = new DataView(b.buffer, b.byteOffset).getUint32(off, true);
371	      st.frameX[idx] = new DataView(b.buffer, b.byteOffset).getUint16(off + 4, true);
372	      st.frameY[idx] = new DataView(b.buffer, b.byteOffset).getUint16(off + 6, true);
373	    }
374	  };
375	  // 金标 wire 直注（TTTT：#101 DeadMans isAGoodSpot 27=27 实证——JS 逻辑 1:1，
376	  // 重放差纯 wire 输入债；.wire 通道 = 织入探针全网格快照）
377	  const injectGoldenWires = (st: InstanceType<typeof TileStore>, wirePath: string) => {
378	    if (!existsSync(wirePath)) return;
379	    const gw = new Uint8Array(readFileSync(wirePath).buffer.slice(0));
380	    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
381	      const g = gw[i]! & 0x1f;
382	      st.wire[i] = (st.wire[i]! & ~0x1f) | g;
383	    }
384	  };
385	  // 金标宝箱注册表（.chest.tsv → Chests；vi→JS id 转换）
386	  const loadGoldenChests = (path: string): Chests | null => {
387	    if (!existsSync(path)) return null;
388	    const vanKey = (vid: number) => VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid];
389	    const gold: Chests = [];
390	    for (const ln of readFileSync(path, 'utf8').trim().split('\n')) {
391	      const f = ln.split('\t');
392	      const items: Chests[number]['items'] = [];
393	      for (let q = 3; q < f.length; q++) {
394	        if (f[q] === '-') { items.push(null); continue; }
395	        const [tid, stack] = f[q]!.split(',').map(Number);
396	        const key = vanKey(tid!);
397	        const jsId = key ? ITEM_BY_KEY[key] : undefined;
398	        items.push(jsId !== undefined ? { id: jsId, stack: stack! } : null);
399	      }
400	      gold.push({ x: Number(f[1]), y: Number(f[2]), items });
401	    }
402	    return gold;
403	  };
404	  if (process.env.SW_WWW_GF !== '0') {
405	    const s51 = P.get(51)!;
406	    s51.steps[0]!.run = (st, gs, rng, chests) => {

410	      // vi 键空间经 VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID→ITEM_BY_KEY 转 JS id）

412	        const vanKey = (vid: number) => VANILLA_ITEM_KEY_BY_ID[vid];
413	        const lines = readFileSync('/tmp/tttt-fr/099_Tile_Cleanup.chest.tsv', 'utf8').trim().split('\n');

446	(RUN ? it : it.skip)(`WWW tail replay ${FROM}..${TO}`, async () => {
447	  const t0 = Date.now();
448	  const producersBox: { shrineSpots: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null; oceanTreasures: Array<{ x: number; y: number }> | null } = { shrineSpots: null, oceanTreasures: null };
449	  const plans = makePlans(producersBox);
450	  const world = new World(W, H, SEED);
451	  const rows: Row[] = [];
452	
453	  const slots = [...plans.keys()].filter(s => {
454	    const ds = plans.get(s)!.steps.map(x => x.dump ?? 999).filter(d => d < 999);
455	    return ds.some(d => d >= FROM && d <= TO);
456	  }).sort((a, b) => a - b);
457	  for (const s of slots) {
458	    const plan = plans.get(s)!;
459	    if (plan.steps.some(x => x.dump === 61 || x.dump === 62) && !producersBox.shrineSpots) {
460	      const producers = computeProducers();
461	      producersBox.shrineSpots = producers.shrineSpots; producersBox.oceanTreasures = producers.oceanTreasures;
462	      console.log(`producers: shrineSpots=${producersBox.shrineSpots.length} oceanTreasures=${producersBox.oceanTreasures.length}`);
463	    }
464	    const entry = boundary(plan.entry);
465	    const gs: GenState = structuredClone(entry.gs);
466	    restoreGenSolid(entry.gensolid);
467	    __setGenStateForProbe(gs);
468	    const chests = structuredClone(entry.chests) as Chests;
469	    let prev: PrevOut | null = null;
470	    for (const step of plan.steps) {
471	      const built = prev ? buildHybrid(step.base, prev) : buildHybrid(step.base, entry);
472	      if (built.revMiss) console.log(`  [revMiss] base=${step.base} cells=${built.revMiss}`);
473	      const rng = new RNG(SEED);
474	      // UUUU：掷骰 span 记录（对拍 vanilla SW_TT_SPAN_PASSES 织入流）。包装对象
475	      // 与 RNG 鸭子类型兼容（run 步内一切嵌套调用同流记录）。
476	      const spanOut = process.env.SW_WWW_SPAN_OUT && process.env.SW_WWW_SPAN_DUMP === String(step.dump)
477	        ? process.env.SW_WWW_SPAN_OUT : undefined;
478	      // FFFFF:SW_WWW_SPAN_TRACK=1 → 掷序×TrackGenerator 逐列步进对照表
479	      // (roll idx → loop/k/x/y/playerHeight 变更行;定位 vanilla span 内嵌的
480	      //  独有掷在 TrackPass 语境中的精确落点)
481	      const trackLog: string[] = [];
482	      let spanRoll = 0;
483	      // FFFFF:JS 侧掷栈迹（SW_WWW_SPAN_JSTACK=from:to,配 SW_WWW_SPAN_TRACK 用）
484	      const jstk = (process.env.SW_WWW_SPAN_JSTACK ?? '').split(':').map(Number);
485	      const jstackLo = jstk.length === 2 && jstk.every(Number.isFinite) ? jstk[0]! : -1;
486	      const jstackHi = jstk.length === 2 && jstk.every(Number.isFinite) ? jstk[1]! : -1;
487	      const jstackLog: string[] = [];
488	      if (spanOut && process.env.SW_WWW_SPAN_TRACK === '1') {
489	        let lastStep = '';
490	        __setTrackStepProbe((loop, k, x, y, ph) => {
491	          const cur = `${loop} k=${k} (${x},${y}) ph=${ph}`;
492	          if (cur !== lastStep) { trackLog.push(`roll ${spanRoll} → ${cur}`); lastStep = cur; }
493	        });
494	        // FFFFF:SW_WWW_TF_SHEET=178,184 → 该 sheet 族 tf314 分派快照(掷位关联)
495	        const tfSheets = new Set((process.env.SW_WWW_TF_SHEET ?? '').split(',').map(Number).filter(Boolean));
496	        if (tfSheets.size) {
497	          const SHEETC = SHEET;
498	          (globalThis as { __tfSt?: InstanceType<typeof TileStore> }).__tfSt = built.st;
499	          __setTfCellProbe((sheet, x, y, fx, fy) => {
500	            if (tfSheets.has(sheet)) {
501	              const nb = (dx: number, dy: number) => { const st = (globalThis as { __tfSt?: InstanceType<typeof TileStore> }).__tfSt!; if (!st) return '?'; if (!st.inBounds(x + dx, y + dy)) return 'OoB'; const i = st.idx(x + dx, y + dy); return st.flags[i] ? `s${SHEETC[st.type[i]!]}sl${st.slope[i]}h${st.half[i]}` : 'air'; };
502	              trackLog.push(`roll ${spanRoll} TF sheet=${sheet} (${x},${y}) fx=${fx} fy=${fy} n(U,D,L,R)=${nb(0, -1)},${nb(0, 1)},${nb(-1, 0)},${nb(1, 0)}`);
503	            }
504	          });
505	        }
506	      }
507	      const spiderLog: string[] = [];
508	      if (process.env.SW_WWW_SPIDER) (globalThis as { __swSpiderVisit?: (cx: number, cy: number, k: string, b: number, a2: number, w: number) => void }).__swSpiderVisit = (cx, cy, k, b, a2, w) => spiderLog.push(`${w} ${k} ${cx},${cy} ${b}:${a2}`);
509	      // IIIII：SW_WWW_SPIDER2=/path → 蜘蛛分支级陈设日志（H/SW/AIR/FLR/CH/POT/CEIL/
510	      // TIGHT/D187/PR3/P1/P0 行，与 vanilla SpiderProbe 分支级织入逐行对拍）
511	      const spiderDeco: string[] = [];
512	      if (process.env.SW_WWW_SPIDER2) (globalThis as { __swSpiderDeco?: (s: string) => void }).__swSpiderDeco = (s) => spiderDeco.push(s);
513	      const rngW = spanOut ? new Proxy(rng, {
514	        get(target, prop, recv) {
515	          const v = Reflect.get(target, prop, target);
516	          // AAAAA：rng.vanilla（UnifiedRandom 直通）——next()=C# 裸 Next() 录 'n'
517	          if (prop === 'vanilla') {
518	            const ur = v as { next(): number };
519	            return { next: () => { const r = ur.next(); if (process.env.SW_WWW_SPAN_DN !== '0') { spanLines.push('n'); spanRoll++; } return r; } };
520	          }
521	          if (typeof v !== 'function') return v;
522	          const fname = String(prop);
523	          return (...args: number[]) => {
524	            const r = (v as (...a: number[]) => number).apply(target, args);
525	            let line = '';
526	            if (fname === 'nextInt') line = `a ${args[0]}`;
527	            else if (fname === 'int') line = `b ${args[0]} ${args[1]! + 1}`;
528	            else if (fname === 'nextIntRange') line = `b ${args[0]} ${args[1]}`;
529	            // AAAAA：全样本流 d/n 通道（对齐 XXXX 织入的 vanilla span 格式——
530	            //   NextDouble()→'d'、裸 Next()→'n'；SW_WWW_SPAN_DN=0 可关）
531	            else if (process.env.SW_WWW_SPAN_DN !== '0' && (fname === 'next' || fname === 'chance' || fname === 'range')) line = 'd';
532	            if (line) {
533	              spanLines.push(line); spanRoll++;
534	              // FFFFF:SW_WWW_SPAN_JSTACK=from:to → 掷区间栈迹(<out>.jstack.txt)
535	              if (jstackLo >= 0 && spanRoll >= jstackLo && spanRoll <= jstackHi) {
536	                jstackLog.push(`roll ${spanRoll} ${line}\n${new Error().stack}\n====`);
537	              }
538	            }
539	            return r;
540	          };
541	        },
542	      }) as RNG : rng;
543	      const spanLines: string[] = [];
544	      const segLog: string[] = [];
545	      if (spanOut) (globalThis as { __swSeg?: (n: string, d?: unknown) => void }).__swSeg = (n, d) => segLog.push(n + ' ' + JSON.stringify(d));
546	      // WWWW：SW_WWW_HOUSE=/path + SW_WWW_HOUSE_DUMP=59 → 小屋序列日志
```

```
257	  const dir = checkMultiDirAnchor(st, x, y);
258	  if (dir < 0) return;                       // CheckAndAdjust 失败：不放置、不掷骰
259	  const roll1 = rng.nextInt(3) * 18;         // ① cs:59724
260	  st.setTile(x, y, EXPOSED, style * 18, roll1);
261	  const roll2 = rng.nextInt(3) * 18;         // ② cs:85855（resetFrame=true 无条件掷）
262	  if (dir !== 0) st.frameY[st.idx(x, y)] = GEM_DIR_BASE[dir] + roll2; // num22 基带覆写
263	  // ② cs:59724 SquareTileFrame：八邻全分派（含非活跃清 half/slope/块漆 + 165/485
264	  // 等锚定装饰帧杀级联——★SSSS：RRRR 帧杀引擎接入，#93 残 10/11 的主根因）。中心
265	  // 178 引擎无分派 case（非实心非仙人掌/藤）= no-op，roll2/roll3 由上方手写保持。
266	  genSquareTileFrame(st, rng, x, y);
267	  rng.nextInt(3);                            // ③ cs:60281 尾部帧（band 内 no-write 幽灵掷）
268	  // ③ cs:60281 尾部 SquareTileFrame：八邻再访（首访击杀后二访级联复核——邻堆连杀/
269	  // 苔藓芽重掷语义，同 PilesPass 双尾帧口径）。
270	  genSquareTileFrame(st, rng, x, y);
271	}
272	
273	// ============ GemCaves 宝石洞（:17528-17572 + gemCave + Spread.Gem 1:1） ============
274	/** Main.tileSolidTop（Main.cs:6764+ 全 86 项，U/EEEE 批同表副本——不 import 防耦合） */
275	const SOLID_TOP = new Set([14, 16, 18, 19, 87, 88, 101, 114, 134, 239, 275, 276, 277, 278, 279, 280, 281,
276	  285, 286, 296, 297, 298, 299, 309, 310, 339, 358, 359, 361, 362, 363, 364, 376, 380, 391, 392, 393, 394,
277	  405, 413, 414, 427, 469, 532, 533, 538, 542, 544, 550, 551, 553, 554, 555, 556, 558, 559, 582, 599, 600,
278	  601, 602, 603, 604, 605, 606, 607, 608, 609, 610, 611, 612, 619, 629, 632, 640, 643, 644, 645, 710]);
279	/** WorldGen.SolidTile（cs:70155-70172）1:1：active && 生成期 tileSolid && !tileSolidTop &&
280	 *  !halfBrick && slope==0 && !inActive；越界走 catch → false。
281	 *  ★G8（2026-08-18 IIII 批）：实心源必须用生成期 Main.tileSolid（vanGenSolidType）——
282	 *  静态 TILE_DEFS.solid 在本槽位与生成期表有 190 荧光蘑菇块/225 蜂巢块/226 蜥蜴砖/
283	 *  481-483 裂砖/659 以太块/484 仙人掌 六族差（UndergroundHouses 尾 cs:17220-23 等翻
284	 *  false），countTiles/Spread.Gem 曾以静态表把"原版可穿"的薄墙当实心 → 洪泛连通域
285	 *  破碎：JS 拒 vanilla 收（腔被割裂）或 JS 收 vanilla 拒（腔局部被困）。#64 首差即
286	 *  site#1 (1765,823) 荧光蘑菇块墙误挡——site#0 八通道逐位全对（流同源）而 site#1
287	 *  起整片错位实证。
288	 *  ★G4（旧案）：SOLID_LUT 只查 def.solid 丢 half/slope/solidTop 的历史教训保持。 */
289	function solidTileAt(st: TileStore, x: number, y: number): boolean {
290	  if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return false;      // 索引越界 → catch → false（cs:70177）
291	  const i = st.idx(x, y);
292	  const t = st.type[i]!;
293	  return st.flags[i] !== 0 && vanGenSolidType(t) && !SOLID_TOP.has(SHEET[t] ?? -1)
294	    && st.half[i] === 0 && st.slope[i] === 0 && ((st.wire[i]! >>> 5) & 1) === 0;
295	}
296	
297	export function runGemCavesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
298	  const g0 = globalThis as { __swGcCap?: (site: number, sx: number, sy: number, tries: number, x: number, y: number, air: number, lava: number, ice: number, rock: number) => void };
299	  // 裸 double 比较（cs:17534：`for i < w*0.003`）——不取整，12.6 也进 13 次
300	  const num = st.w * 0.003;
301	  // nextCount（WorldGen.cs:9506-9581）：洪泛连通空气腔；wall!=0 即封顶；lavaOk=false
302	  // 时岩浆格 lavaCount++ 且立即封顶（含空气格岩浆）；ice 只认 147/161
303	  // ★零风险优化(2026-08-13):原 Set<number>+元组数组 → 平坦 Int32Array 栈 +
304	  // Uint8Array seen(visited 列表局部清除)。push 四邻顺序与 pop 语义逐点保持
305	  // (DFS 下邻优先),掷骰/判定序列不变。
306	  const seenArr = new Uint8Array(st.w * st.h);
307	  const visited: number[] = [];
308	  const stackX = new Int32Array(4096);
309	  const stackY = new Int32Array(4096);
310	  const countTiles = (sx: number, sy: number): { air: number; lava: number; ice: number; rock: number } => {
311	    let air = 0, lava = 0, ice = 0, rock = 0;
312	    let sp = 0;
313	    stackX[sp] = sx; stackY[sp] = sy; sp++;
314	    while (sp > 0) {
315	      if (air >= 300) break;                                  // numTileCount >= maxTileCount(300)
316	      sp--;
317	      const x = stackX[sp], y = stackY[sp];
318	      if (x <= 1 || x >= st.w - 1 || y <= 1 || y >= st.h - 1) {
319	        for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
320	        visited.length = 0;
321	        return { air: 300, lava, ice, rock };
322	      }
323	      const ii = st.idx(x, y);
324	      if (seenArr[ii]) continue;
325	      seenArr[ii] = 1;
326	      visited.push(ii);
327	      if (st.wall[ii] === 244) {
328	        for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
329	        visited.length = 0;
330	        return { air: 300, lava, ice, rock };
331	      }
332	      if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === LIQUID_TYPE.SHIMMER) {
333	        for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
334	        visited.length = 0;
335	        return { air: 300, lava, ice, rock };
336	      }
337	      if (st.wall[ii] !== 0) {
338	        for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
339	        visited.length = 0;
340	        return { air: 300, lava, ice, rock };                  // jungle=false
341	      }
342	      if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA) {
343	        lava++;
344	        for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
345	        visited.length = 0;
346	        return { air: 300, lava, ice, rock };
347	      }
348	      if (st.flags[ii]) { // 实心：只计数不扩散
349	        if (ICE_COUNT.has(st.type[ii])) ice++;
350	        else if (st.type[ii] === STONE) rock++;
351	      }
352	      if (!solidTileAt(st, x, y)) {           // cs:9584 !SolidTile(x,y) → 计数+扩散
353	        air++;
354	        // 四邻入栈(与原 push 顺序一致,pop 取尾 = 下邻先访)。
355	        // 栈深上界 = 4×300(air≤300,超即 break,仅 air 格入栈)+1 < 4096,无需增长
356	        stackX[sp] = x - 1; stackY[sp] = y; sp++;
357	        stackX[sp] = x + 1; stackY[sp] = y; sp++;
358	        stackX[sp] = x; stackY[sp] = y - 1; sp++;
359	        stackX[sp] = x; stackY[sp] = y + 1; sp++;
360	      }
361	    }
362	    for (const v of visited) seenArr[v] = 0;
363	    visited.length = 0;
364	    return { air, lava, ice, rock };
365	  };
366	  for (let i = 0; i < num; i++) {
367	    // 选点（:17547-17565）：x∈[200,w-200)、y∈[rockLevel+30, h-230)、
368	    // countTiles∈[50,300) 且 lavaCount==0 && iceCount==0 && rockCount>0（1000 次上限）
369	    let sx = -1, sy = -1;
370	    for (let tries = 0; tries < 1000; tries++) {
371	      const x = rng.nextIntRange(200, st.w - 200);
372	      const y = rng.nextIntRange(gs.rockLevel + 30, st.h - 230);
373	      const c = countTiles(x, y);
374	      g0.__swGcCap?.(i, x, y, tries, x, y, c.air, c.lava, c.ice, c.rock);
375	      if (c.air >= 50 && c.air < 300 && c.lava === 0 && c.ice === 0 && c.rock > 0) { sx = x; sy = y; break; }
376	    }
377	    if (sx < 0) continue;
378	    // 选池（gemCave :9657-9673）：强制 1 种 + 各 1/6 追加（1~7 种）
379	    const gemOn = new Set<number>([rng.nextInt(6)]);
380	    for (let k = 0; k < 6; k++) if (rng.nextInt(6) === 0) gemOn.add(k);
381	    const randGem = () => { let n = rng.nextInt(6); while (!gemOn.has(n)) n = rng.nextInt(6); return n; };
382	    // randGemTile（:9685-9700）：19/20 石头，1/20 池内宝石
383	    const randGemTileId = () => (rng.nextInt(20) !== 0 ? STONE : GEM_TILES[randGem()]);
384	    // Spread.Gem（:3565-3651）1:1 BFS：
385	    //  实心格（或有墙空气格）→ 不扩散；仅 active 且 Gemmable 时自身+四邻转 randGemTile
386	    //  （空气+有墙格：if(tile.active()) 内零操作零掷，cs:3599-3619）；
387	    //  无墙空气格 → 墙 48+池选、1/2 暴露晶簇、四邻入队（神庙/房屋墙阻隔，天然不侵入）
388	    const seen = new Set<number>();
389	    let frontier: Array<[number, number]> = [[sx, sy]];
390	    while (frontier.length) {
391	      const next: Array<[number, number]> = [];
392	      for (const [x, y] of frontier) {
393	        // InWorld(x,y,1)（cs:3572：x<1||x>=w-1||y<1||y>=h-1 才出界——x==1 合法；
394	        //  countTiles 的 nextCount 边界是 x<=1（cs:9513），两处口径不同勿混）
395	        if (x < 1 || x >= st.w - 1 || y < 1 || y >= st.h - 1) continue;
396	        const ii = st.idx(x, y);
397	        // ★原版无处理时去重（cs:3605 hashSet.Add 幂等，无 Contains 短路）：同波双亲
398	        // 推入的重复点会被二次处理——纯空气格二访因首访已写 wall≠0 落入实心支
399	        // !active 早退（零掷 no-op）；但 active 格二访（含实心 Gemmable 与有墙非实心
400	        // 植物）会再次消费 randGemTile/四邻转换掷——曾 `if (seen.has(ii)) continue`
401	        // 吞掉这些掷，RNG 流分叉（#64 首差 W(3805,614) 族 2026-08-18）。
402	        seen.add(ii);
403	        if (solidTileAt(st, x, y) || st.wall[ii] !== 0) { // cs:3597 SolidTile||wall!=0
404	          if (!st.flags[ii]) continue;                    // 空气+有墙：零操作零掷
405	          if (GEMMABLE.has(st.type[ii])) st.type[ii] = randGemTileId();
406	          for (const [dx, dy] of [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]] as const) {
407	            const nx = x + dx, ny = y + dy;
408	            if (!st.inBounds(nx, ny)) continue;
409	            const ni = st.idx(nx, ny);
410	            if (st.flags[ni] && GEMMABLE.has(st.type[ni])) st.type[ni] = randGemTileId();
411	          }
412	        } else {
413	          st.wall[ii] = GEM_WALL_BASE + randGem();
414	          // cs:3604：放置前另有 !tile.active() 门（&& 左求值，active 时不掷骰）——
415	          // 分支谓词改 SolidTile 后，半砖/坡/平台等 active 非实心格也会落到本分支，
416	          // 此门不可省（否则会覆盖活性格）
417	          if (!st.flags[ii] && rng.nextInt(2) === 0) placeExposed(st, rng, x, y, randGem());
418	          for (const [dx, dy] of [[-1, 0], [1, 0], [0, -1], [0, 1]] as const) {
419	            const ni = st.idx(x + dx, y + dy);
420	            if (!seen.has(ni)) next.push([x + dx, y + dy]);
421	          }
422	        }
423	      }
424	      frontier = next;
425	    }
426	  }
427	}
428	
429	// ============ ExposedGems 暴露晶簇（:20842-20913） ============
430	// ★原版是【两个独立 GenPass】：ExposedGemsInIceBiome(:20842-20852+) 与
431	//   ExposedGemsUnderground(:20874-20913)——RunPass 每 pass 前重播种子
432	//   （WorldGenerator.cs:497），地下/琥珀段从【新流】起掷。曾合并为一函数一气流
433	//   → #93 掷流错位（WWW 批 2026-08-18 拆分；WorldGen.vanillaGems 槽内两调重播）。
434	export function runExposedGemsIcePass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
435	  // 冰系（:20842-20872）：y∈[(ws+rl)/2, lavaLine)、x=逐行雪界 snowMinX/MaxX[y]；
436	  // 目标格自身为冰块（147/161/162/224）→ 先掷 4 个 Next(1,4) 窗口再掷 Next(12) style
437	  // （cs:20855-20859 掷序），周边不对称窗口的空气格放晶簇
438	  for (let i = 0; i < st.w * 0.25; i++) {
439	    const y = rng.nextIntRange(Math.floor((gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2), gs.lavaLine);
440	    if (y < 0 || y >= st.h) continue;
441	    const minX = gs.snowMinX?.[y] ?? gs.snowOriginLeft;
442	    const maxX = gs.snowMaxX?.[y] ?? gs.snowOriginRight;
443	    // cs:20851：x 掷骰**无条件**（snowMinX/MaxX 只填到 lavaLine-140，未填带读默认 0
444	    // 仍掷，Next(0,0)=0）——旧 `maxX<=minX continue` 吞掉必掷一骰，RNG 流分叉
445	    const x = rng.nextIntRange(minX, maxX);
446	    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
447	    if (!st.flags[ii] || !ICE_FLOOR.has(st.type[ii])) continue;
448	    const wl = 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
449	    const wr = 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
450	    const wt = 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
451	    const wb = 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
452	    const style = randGemStyle(rng);
453	    for (let jx = x - wl; jx < x + wr; jx++) {
454	      for (let jy = y - wt; jy < y + wb; jy++) {
455	        if (jx < 40 || jx >= st.w - 40 || jy < 40 || jy >= st.h - 40) continue;
456	        const ji = st.idx(jx, jy);
457	        if (!st.flags[ji]) placeExposed(st, rng, jx, jy, style);
458	      }
459	    }
460	  }
461	}
462	
463	/** ExposedGemsUnderground（cs:20874-20913）独立 pass：地下单格 + 琥珀簇两段 */
464	export function runExposedGemsUndergroundPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
465	  // 地下（:20874-20890）：w 次，x∈[20,w-20)、y∈[rockLevel, h-300)，空气+无岩浆+
466	  // 非地牢墙且墙≠27 → 单格（style=Next(12) 权重）——ExposedGemsUnderground 独立 pass
467	  // （新流起掷，与冰系段无关）
468	  for (let i = 0; i < st.w; i++) {
469	    const x = rng.nextIntRange(20, st.w - 20);
470	    const y = rng.nextIntRange(gs.rockLevel, st.h - 300);
471	    if (y < 0) continue;
472	    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
473	    if (st.flags[ii]) continue;
474	    if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA) continue; // anyLava（+1 编码）
475	    const wl = st.wall[ii];
476	    if (wl === 27 || WALL_DUNGEON.has(wl)) continue;
477	    placeExposed(st, rng, x, y, randGemStyle(rng));
478	  }
479	  // 琥珀（:20891-20913）：w 次，y∈[ws, h-300)，目标须空气+无岩浆+墙 187/216 →
480	  // 不对称窗口（各 1-3）的空气格放 style=6 晶簇。
481	  // 目标的 !active+!anyLava 门（cs:20901）曾缺失——实心沙岩也带 187/216 墙，
482	  // 命中率高一个数量级 → 琥珀簇 13× 金标（2026-08-12 用户标注）。
483	  for (let i = 0; i < st.w; i++) {
484	    const x = rng.nextIntRange(20, st.w - 20);
485	    const y = rng.nextIntRange(gs.worldSurface, st.h - 300);
486	    if (y < 0) continue;
487	    const ii = st.idx(x, y);
488	    if (st.flags[ii]) continue;
489	    if (st.liquid[ii] > 0 && st.liquidType[ii] === LIQUID_TYPE.LAVA) continue; // anyLava（+1 编码，勿写 0）
490	    const wl = st.wall[ii];
491	    if (wl !== 187 && wl !== 216) continue;
492	    const n14 = () => 1 + rng.nextInt(3);
493	    const al = n14(), ar = n14(), at = n14(), ab = n14();
494	    for (let jx = x - al; jx < x + ar; jx++) {
495	      for (let jy = y - at; jy < y + ab; jy++) {
496	        if (!st.inBounds(jx, jy)) continue;
497	        const ji = st.idx(jx, jy);
498	        if (!st.flags[ji]) placeExposed(st, rng, jx, jy, 6);
499	      }
500	    }
501	  }
502	}
503	
504	/** 旧整段入口（冰+地下+琥珀一气流）——保留仅为兼容探针引用；管线走拆分两段+槽内重播 */
505	export function runExposedGemsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
506	  runExposedGemsIcePass(st, rng, gs);
507	  runExposedGemsUndergroundPass(st, rng, gs);
508	}
509	
510	// ============ SpeleothemsAndGemTrees 钟乳石+宝石树（:22196-22282） ============
511	export function runGemTreesPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState): void {
512	  const g0 = globalThis as { __swSpCap?: (gs: GenState, st: TileStore) => void; __swScanCap?: (x: number, y: number, phase: number) => void };
513	  try { g0.__swSpCap?.(gs, st); } catch { /* probe */ }
514	  const f = gs.seedFlags ?? {};
515	  const alwaysTry = !!(f.tenthAnniversary || f.drunkWorld);
516	  const oceanDepths = (x: number, y: number): boolean => {
517	    if (y > (gs.worldSurface + gs.rockLevel) / 2 + 40) return false;
518	    return x < gs.beachDistance || x > st.w - gs.beachDistance;
519	  };
520	  const sheetActive = (x: number, y: number): number => {
521	    if (!st.inBounds(x, y)) return -1;
522	    const i = st.idx(x, y);
523	    return st.flags[i] ? SHEET[st.type[i]] : -1;
524	  };
525	  const rockFamily = (s: number): boolean =>
526	    STONE_FAMILY.has(s) || TILE_MOSS.has(s);
527	  for (let x = 20; x < st.w - 20; x++) {
528	    for (let y = Math.floor(gs.worldSurface); y < st.h - 20; y++) {
529	      g0.__swScanCap?.(x, y, 0);
530	      // 宝石树段（:22208-22237）：掷骰在液体判定之前（短路与求值序 1:1）
531	      const treeRoll = alwaysTry ? true : rng.nextInt(5) === 0;
532	      if (treeRoll && st.liquid[st.idx(x, y - 1)] === 0) {
533	        const treeType = idsByVanillaSheet(583 + rng.nextInt(7)).values().next().value ?? 0;
534	        if (treeType) growGemTree(st, rng, x, y, treeType);
535	      }
536	      // 地下钟乳石（:22239-22262）：非海洋、空格、1/5 → 上下邻石族清坡 + PlaceTight
537	      if (!oceanDepths(x, y) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && rng.nextInt(5) === 0) {
538	        const above = sheetActive(x, y - 1), below = sheetActive(x, y + 1);
539	        if (rockFamily(above) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y + 1)]) {
540	          st.slope[st.idx(x, y - 1)] = 0;
541	        }
542	        if (rockFamily(below) && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y)] && !st.flags[st.idx(x, y - 1)]) {
543	          st.slope[st.idx(x, y + 1)] = 0;
544	        }
545	        placeTightInitial(st, rng, x, y);
546	      }
547	    }
548	    // 地表钟乳石（:22264-22278）：k∈[5, worldSurface)；冰上 147/161、沙 25/203 上/下
549	    for (let k = 5; k < gs.worldSurface; k++) {
550	      g0.__swScanCap?.(x, k, 1);
551	      const up = sheetActive(x, k - 1), down = sheetActive(x, k + 1);
552	      const clearAbove = () => {
553	        if (!st.flags[st.idx(x, k)] && !st.flags[st.idx(x, k + 1)]) st.slope[st.idx(x, k - 1)] = 0;
554	      };
555	      if ((up === 147 || up === 161) && rng.nextInt(5) === 0) {
556	        clearAbove();
557	        placeTightInitial(st, rng, x, k);
558	      }
559	      if ((up === 25 || up === 203) && rng.nextInt(5) === 0) {
560	        clearAbove();
561	        placeTightInitial(st, rng, x, k);
562	      }
```

```
}
/** PPPP：跨 pass 复用的生成期帧链（WorldUtils 侧 frameNeighbors 支的 1:1 入口）。
 *  vanilla 用 Actions.SetTile（setSelfFrames+setNeighborFrames 默认真）与
 *  Actions.ClearTile(frameNeighbors:true) 的所有 Gen 步骤，其邻帧 WorldGen.TileFrame
 *  会分派 frameImportant Check* 击杀链——典型：蜂蜜斑挖蜜池 ClearTile 四邻帧打到
 *  下方祭坛格 → Check3x2(26) 支撑失守 → KillTile×6 整坛坍塌（12345 链 #47 债）。
 *  - 'clearTile'：WorldUtils.ClearTile（cs:55-64）——先清本格（调用方自理）再四邻
 *    +x/-x/+y/-y，resetFrame=false；
 *  - 'setTile'：WorldUtils.TileFrame（cs:79-92）——中心 resetFrame=true 在前，
 *    四邻 resetFrame=true 在后。 */
export function genFrameNeighbors(
  st: TileStore, rng: RNG,
  x: number, y: number,
  mode: 'clearTile' | 'setTile',
): void {
  const ctx: SweepCtx = { rng, destroyObject: false, frameSparse: false, stats: undefined };
  if (mode === 'clearTile') {
    tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x + 1, y, false);
    tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x - 1, y, false);
    tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x, y + 1, false);
    tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x, y - 1, false);
  } else {
    tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x, y, true);
    tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x + 1, y, true);
    tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x - 1, y, true);
    tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x, y + 1, true);
    tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x, y - 1, true);
  }
}

/** SquareTileFrame 生成期（cs:80924，九宫、仅中心 resetFrame=true）——
 *  FrameOutAllHiveContents 的 tile 侧等消费端（cs:22544 同构邻格击杀级联） */
export function genSquareTileFrame(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): void {
  const ctx: SweepCtx = { rng, destroyObject: false, frameSparse: false, stats: undefined };
  squareTileFrame(st, ctx, x, y);
}

/** UUUU 批新增纯导出：WorldGen.TileFrame(i, j, resetFrame:true) 单格生成期入口
 *  （DeadMansChest ActuallyPlace* 族的字面镜像——cs:532/570/617 直调单格 TileFrame
 *  而非 SquareTileFrame）。零行为新增：仅包装私有 tileFrameGen。 */
export function genTileFrame(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number): void {
  const ctx: SweepCtx = { rng, destroyObject: false, frameSparse: false, stats: undefined };
  tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x, y, true);
}

const roll3 = (ctx: SweepCtx): number => {
  if (ctx.stats) ctx.stats.roll184++;
  return ctx.rng ? ctx.rng.int(0, 2) : 0;
};
const rollRange = (ctx: SweepCtx, min: number, maxIncl: number): number => {
  if (ctx.stats) ctx.stats.rollsOther++;
  return ctx.rng ? ctx.rng.int(min, maxIncl) : min;
};

/** TileFrame 生成期（cs:82067） */
function tileFrameGen(st: TileStore, ctx: SweepCtx, x: number, y: number, reset: boolean): void {
  // 边距 5 早退（cs:82070）；!active 清 half/slope/块漆早退（cs:82078-82082，
  // ClearBlockPaintAndCoating——RRRR 补块漆清）
  if (x <= 5 || y <= 5 || x >= st.w - 5 || y >= st.h - 5) return;
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  if (!st.flags[i]) { st.half[i] = 0; st.slope[i] = 0; st.paint[i] = 0; return; }
  const sheet = SWEEP_SHEET[st.type[i]];
  if (sheet < 0) return;
  if (frameImportantDispatch(st, ctx, x, y, sheet, reset)) return;
  // 非实心非 frameImportant：仙人掌 / 藤（cs:82102-82117）
  const d = TILE_DEFS[st.type[i]];
  if (!d?.solid) {
    if (sheet === 80) { if (checkCactusSweep(st, ctx, x, y)) return; }
    else if (IS_VINE.has(sheet)) { checkVinesSweep(st, ctx, x, y, sheet); return; }
  }
}

/** TileFrameImportant 分派（cs:85703+）——返回 true=已处理（含 no-op 族） */
function frameImportantDispatch(st: TileStore, ctx: SweepCtx, x: number, y: number, sheet: number, reset = false): boolean {
  // frameSparse 模式跳过的读帧族（165/185/28/240族/485/72/C3X2 族——见 SweepCtx 注释）
  //   ★CCCCC：+138/411——Check2x2 完整性门读 2×2 四格帧；探针基座（金标 typ×陈旧
  //   JS 帧嵌合）上巨石/起爆器帧不可信 → 锚反解到互指的死区 → 尾 4×4 帧与 184 杀
  //   级联互喂成无限递归（#105 实爆）。生产（frameSparse=false，帧由本管线同源写
  //   入）恒自洽不受影响；#101 的 genTileFrame 直调 ctx 同为 false（TrackPass 巷杀
  //   级联实证无爆）。
  if (ctx.frameSparse && (
    sheet === 165 || sheet === 185 || sheet === 28 || sheet === 653 || sheet === 240
    || sheet === 440 || sheet === 485 || sheet === 72 || sheet === 178 || sheet === 138
    || sheet === 411 || C3X2_TYPES.has(sheet))) {
    return true;   // UUUU：178 同列读帧族（sparse 模式下嵌合帧会伪杀 CheckAndAdjust）
  }
  switch (sheet) {
    case 518: checkLilyPadSweep(st, ctx, x, y); return true;
    case 519: checkCatTailSweep(st, ctx, x, y); return true;
    case 549: checkSeaweedSweep(st, ctx, x, y); return true;
    case 571: checkBambooSweep(st, ctx, x, y); return true;
    case 72: frame72Sweep(st, ctx, x, y); return true;
    case 184: frame184Sweep(st, ctx, x, y); return true;
    case 165:
      // ShimmerPass 1:1（UpdateStalagtiteStyle 掷+杀）；reframe 钩子改走本扫全分派
      // （击杀级联的邻格 184 等须掷骰重查——彼时 165-only 的 frame165 不再等价）
      if (ctx.rng) {
        checkStalactite(st, ctx.rng, x, y, (s2, _r, xx, yy) => tileFrameGen(s2, ctx, xx, yy, false));
      }
      return true;
    case 3: case 24: case 61: case 71: case 73: case 74:
    case 110: case 113: case 201: case 637: case 703:
      plantCheckSweep(st, ctx, x, y, sheet); return true;
    case 13: case 33: case 49: case 50: case 78: case 174: case 372: case 646: case 707:
      checkOnTableSweep(st, ctx, x, y, sheet); return true;
    case 185: checkPileSweep(st, ctx, x, y); return true;
    case 28: case 653: checkPotSweep(st, ctx, x, y, sheet); return true;
```

```
function killTileGen(st: TileStore, ctx: SweepCtx, x: number, y: number): void {

  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  if (!st.flags[i]) return;
  const sheet = SWEEP_SHEET[st.type[i]];
  const fx = st.frameX[i]!;

  for (let k = 0; k < dust; k++) makeTileDustRoll(ctx, sheet, fx);

}

function squareTileFrame(st: TileStore, ctx: SweepCtx, x: number, y: number): void {

  tileFrameGen(st, ctx, x - 1, y, false);
```

```
}

/** case 178 宝石籽（cs:85844-85886）：CheckAndAdjustMultiDirectionalTile
 *  （cs:60338——四向锚 ValidAnchor+方向优先 下>左>右>上）成功 → 带外帧
 *  frameY = 带基 + (resetFrame ? Next(3)*18 : frameY%54)；失败 → KillTile。 */
function frame178Sweep(st: TileStore, ctx: SweepCtx, x: number, y: number, reset: boolean): void {
  const i = st.idx(x, y);
  const frameY2 = st.frameY[i]!;
  // CheckAndAdjustMultiDirectionalTile：InWorld(i,j,2) 门
  if (!(x >= 2 && y >= 2 && x < st.w - 2 && y < st.h - 2)) { killTileGen(st, ctx, x, y); return; }
  const anchorOk = (ax: number, ay: number, dir: number): boolean => {
    if (!st.inBounds(ax, ay)) return false;
    const k = st.idx(ax, ay);
    const sh = SWEEP_SHEET[st.type[k]!] ?? -1;
    if (sh < 0 || BOULDER_SHEETS.has(sh) || sh === 10) return false;
    if (dir === 0) return solidAllowTop(st, ax, ay);
    if (dir === 1) return solidAllowBottom(st, ax, ay);
    if (dir === 2) return solidAllowSide(st, ax, ay, 'L');
    return solidAllowSide(st, ax, ay, 'R');
  };
  const solidNS = (ax: number, ay: number): boolean => {
    const k = st.idx(ax, ay);
    return genSolidType(st.type[k]!) && !TILE_DEFS[st.type[k]!]?.platform;
  };
  let dir = -1;
  if (anchorOk(x, y + 1, 1) && solidNS(x, y + 1)) dir = 0;          // 下方锚 → 带基 0
  else if (anchorOk(x - 1, y, 2) && solidNS(x - 1, y)) dir = 2;     // 左锚 → 108
  else if (anchorOk(x + 1, y, 3) && solidNS(x + 1, y)) dir = 3;     // 右锚 → 162
  else if (anchorOk(x, y - 1, 0) && solidNS(x, y - 1)) dir = 1;     // 上锚 → 54
  if (dir < 0) { killTileGen(st, ctx, x, y); return; }
  const num21 = (!reset) ? frameY2 % 54 : (ctx.rng ? ctx.rng.nextInt(3) * 18 : 0);
  let num22 = -1;
  if (dir === 0 && (frameY2 < 0 || frameY2 > 36)) num22 = 0;
  else if (dir === 1 && (frameY2 < 54 || frameY2 > 90)) num22 = 54;
  else if (dir === 2 && (frameY2 < 108 || frameY2 > 144)) num22 = 108;
  else if (dir === 3 && (frameY2 < 162 || frameY2 > 198)) num22 = 162;
  if (num22 > -1) st.frameY[i] = num22 + num21;
}

/** case 129/149（cs:85727-85752）：CheckAndAdjustMultiDirectionalTile（cs:60338）失败杀 */
function frame129Sweep(st: TileStore, ctx: SweepCtx, x: number, y: number): void {
  if (!(x >= 2 && y >= 2 && x < st.w - 2 && y < st.h - 2)) { killTileGen(st, ctx, x, y); return; }
  const valid = (xx: number, yy: number, dir: number): number => {
    if (!st.inBounds(xx, yy)) return -1;
    const ki = st.idx(xx, yy);
    const sh = SWEEP_SHEET[st.type[ki]];
    if (sh < 0 || BOULDER_SHEETS.has(sh) || sh === 10) return -1;
    if (dir === 0 && !solidAllowTop(st, xx, yy)) return -1;
    if (dir === 1 && !solidAllowBottom(st, xx, yy)) return -1;
    if (dir === 2 && !solidAllowSide(st, xx, yy, 'L')) return -1;
    if (dir === 3 && !solidAllowSide(st, xx, yy, 'R')) return -1;
    return sh;
  };
  const up = valid(x, y - 1, 0), below = valid(x, y + 1, 1);
  const leftT = valid(x - 1, y, 2), rightT = valid(x + 1, y, 3);
  const solidNS = (sh: number): boolean => {
    const id = REV_SHEET_ID.get(sh);
    return id !== undefined && genSolidType(id) && !TILE_DEFS[id]?.platform;
  };
  const ok = (below >= 0 && solidNS(below)) || (leftT >= 0 && solidNS(leftT))
    || (rightT >= 0 && solidNS(rightT)) || (up >= 0 && solidNS(up));
  if (!ok) killTileGen(st, ctx, x, y);
}

/** case 529（cs:85954-85970）：下须实心底坡容许且沙族，否则杀 */
function frame529Sweep(st: TileStore, ctx: SweepCtx, x: number, y: number): void {
  if (!solidAllowBottom(st, x, y + 1)) { killTileGen(st, ctx, x, y); return; }
  const g = activeAt2(st, x, y + 1) ? sheetAt2(st, x, y + 1) : -1;
  if (g < 0 || !(g === 53 || g === 112 || g === 116 || g === 234)) killTileGen(st, ctx, x, y);
}
```

````
export function placeExposed(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, x: number, y: number, style: number): void {
  const pi = st.idx(x, y);
  if (!st.flags[pi]) { ...clear... }
  const dir = checkMultiDirAnchor(st, x, y);
  if (dir < 0) return;
  const roll1 = rng.nextInt(3) * 18;
  st.setTile(x, y, EXPOSED, style * 18, roll1);
  const roll2 = rng.nextInt(3) * 18;
  if (dir !== 0) st.frameY[st.idx(x, y)] = GEM_DIR_BASE[dir] + roll2;
  genSquareTileFrame(st, rng, x, y);
  rng.nextInt(3);
  genSquareTileFrame(st, rng, x, y);
}
```
````

```
	public static void SquareTileFrame(int i, int j, bool resetFrame = true)
	{
		TileFrame(i - 1, j - 1);
		TileFrame(i - 1, j);
		TileFrame(i - 1, j + 1);
		TileFrame(i, j - 1);
		TileFrame(i, j, resetFrame);
		TileFrame(i, j + 1);
		TileFrame(i + 1, j - 1);
		TileFrame(i + 1, j);
		TileFrame(i + 1, j + 1);
	}

	public static void SquareWallFrame(int i, int j, bool resetFrame = true)
	{
		Framing.WallFrame(i - 1, j - 1);
		Framing.WallFrame(i - 1, j);
		Framing.WallFrame(i - 1, j + 1);
		Framing.WallFrame(i, j - 1);
		Framing.WallFrame(i, j, resetFrame);
		Framing.WallFrame(i, j + 1);
		Framing.WallFrame(i + 1, j - 1);
		Framing.WallFrame(i + 1, j);
		Framing.WallFrame(i + 1, j + 1);
	}

	public static void SectionTileFrameWithCheck(int startX, int startY, int endX, int endY)
```

```
function killTileDustAmount(sheet: number, fx: number): number {
  if (sheet === 654 || sheet === 630 || sheet === 631) return 0;
  if (sheet === 125 || sheet === 138 || sheet === 172 || sheet === 287
    || (sheet >= 300 && sheet <= 308) || sheet === 354 || sheet === 355
    || (sheet >= 373 && sheet <= 376) || sheet === 461 || sheet === 484 || sheet === 499
    || sheet === 564 || sheet === 565 || sheet === 593 || sheet === 594 || sheet === 617
    || sheet === 664 || sheet === 665 || sheet === 699 || sheet === 709
    || (sheet >= 711 && sheet <= 716)) return 0;
  if (sheet === 231) return 6;
  if (sheet >= 481 && sheet <= 483) return 3;
  if (sheet === 541) return 1;
  if (sheet === 549) return 2;
  if (sheet === 534 || sheet === 536 || sheet === 539 || sheet === 625 || sheet === 627) return 5;
  if (sheet === 184) return Math.floor(fx / 22) >= 5 ? 5 : 10;
  return 10;
}
```

```
**任务**: 9293480 链 #64 Gem_Caves 漂移（A=317）归因并清零；OOOO 曾清到八通道零，今日复跑回归。

### ⓪ 定位：金标基座反事实（SW_WWW=rep 64..64）→ pass 自差
- 现网态重放：golden63 基座 + 现网 runGemCavesPass → A=317/T=953/W=2763（首差 (1969,592)，pairs T0>178×162/T178>0×155/T1>0×101/T0>1×89/T1>59×51…全站错位）——与全链 #64 A=317 同值 → **pass 自差**（非输入债），GemPasses 本体或其依赖回归。
- FFFFF SmoothSlope 写坡、StructuresPass/HellFortPass 并行编辑均排除（金标基座隔离了上游输入；solidTileAt 读的是基座态）。

### ① 归因：UUUU 引擎 case 178 × placeExposed 手写镜像双计
- 肇事者：**UUUU 批 ④-4「引擎 case 178 宝石籽」**（FinalCleanupPass.frameImportantDispatch 新增 `case 178: frame178Sweep(...,reset)`，resetFrame=true 时 1×Next(3)+方向基带覆写——cs:85844-85886 字面镜像，本身正确）。
- 机制：GemPasses.placeExposed 的 roll2/roll3 与基带覆写是**引擎无 178 分派时代的补偿镜像**（注释原文"中心 178 引擎无分派 case = no-op，roll2/roll3 由上方手写保持"）——UUUU 上线分派后该假设作废：每次 genSquareTileFrame 的中心访（列主序第 5 位 reset=true）都进 frame178Sweep 再掷一骰 → 每颗宝石 5×Next(3)（vanilla 3×）= **+2 幽灵掷**；首个放置后全 pass 流移位 → 全站错位（T0>178/T178>0 等量互换族）。
- 隐匿机制（为何三日无人察觉）：UUUU 尾段全扫把 #64/65/92/93/103-105 红全记"并行会话在途漂移带（GemPasses 03:16 mtime 实证）"——03:16 实为 **OOOO 清零批自身的落盘时刻**，误读为并行在途；WWWW/IIIII 沿用"GemPasses 并行漂移带既有债（WWWW 期 A=319）"记载。mtime 考古教训：**mtime 新 ≠ 该文件是肇事者——先金标基座反事实分流"输入债 vs pass 自差"再定责**。
- 连带澄清：UUUU 补 sparse 表（frameSparse 跳读 178）只救了槽 57（final cleanup 重放）路径，生产路径（frameSparse=false）双计全量存在。

### ② 修复（GemPasses.ts 单文件，引擎零动——KKKK/UUUU 域不触）
按原语义修正而非回退：引擎 frame178Sweep 即 vanilla TileFrameImportant case 178 的真身，手写镜像退役——
- 删 manual roll2+GEM_DIR_BASE 基带覆写、删 manual roll3（各自改由两次 genSquareTileFrame 的中心访产出，列主序第 5 位=vanilla cs:80924 同序）；
- 尾帧加 `if (st.flags[pi])` 门（cs:60275 `if (tile.active())`——首帧中心被杀 vanilla 跳过尾帧；旧码恒跑）；
- checkMultiDirAnchor 保留为 PlaceTile 前置门（失败不放置不掷骰，cs:59719）。
净效果：每颗宝石掷数 5→3（=vanilla），帧带由引擎 CheckAndAdjust 方向产出。
```
